# League table of ways of earning - Friday 20 November 2026, 14:05 UTC

**Ways tested: 3,299. Ways passing (300 or more filled orders and profit in both halves): 11. Ways on forward paper: 11. New ways this week (from 16 Nov): 381 from traders' methods (a), 3059 from our own data (b), 55 from other ideas (c).**

Every row went through the same test: real prices on offer with the money shown there (at least £2), offers filled only by later trades that went through the price and only for the money that traded, £50 a bet and £50 lay liability at most, 2% commission on each market's net winnings, an exit that could not fill held to the result, stale quotes not used. £ a month = the money made over all the days of data the way could have traded, times 30.4. Halves are by date. Paper only: nothing here is real money.
Data: Betfair 20 days (2026-10-31 to 2026-11-19); Matchbook 17 days (2026-11-03 to 2026-11-19); Smarkets 17 days (2026-11-03 to 2026-11-19). Days tested so far in this run: 8 of 20 (the loop carries on; each way's £ a month uses only the days tested for it). That is about three weeks at most, so most ways do not have 300 orders yet; they are shown with their real count.

Sources: a = how proven exchange traders are documented to earn; b = what our own recorded data shows (history-file slices enter only as ideas and are tested here at real prices); c = anything else. Recorded-grid slices with fewer than 100 bets so far (19,398 of them) are not registered as ways yet; they join automatically as their days add up.

Rows can overlap: several ways may be the same bets described differently (for example laying football selections priced at a 20-30% chance before the start, which is mostly laying the draw). Do not add their £ a month together; a passing way is only proven once its forward paper passes the gate.
